Nothing is written before this point, so returning 412 here costs the user\n// only a re-run, never a partial save.\n//\n// It sits late on purpose (after credit limit, picking, packing and per-line qty validation) so we\n// only ask about a GD that is genuinely about to be saved -- unlike the GR gate, which sits at the\n// top and can prompt for a GD that later fails validation.\nconst saveAs = {{workflowparams:saveAs}};\nconst autoCompletedSi = Number({{node:code_picking_defaults.data.autoCompletedSi}}) || 0;\nconst autoSiConfirmed = {{workflowparams:auto_si_confirmed}};\nconst autoSiSkip = {{workflowparams:auto_si_skip}};\nconst isPicking = {{workflowparams:isPicking}};\nconst isPacking = {{workflowparams:isPacking}};\n\n// The client sends these as `data.auto_si_confirmed || \"\"`, so they arrive as true or \"\".\nconst truthy = (v) =>\n  v === true || v === 1 || v === \"true\" || v === \"1\" || v === \"Yes\";\n\n// Deliberately NOT gated on auto-GR.
